  253  *   process empty document table
  254  *
  255  * - the length of sum fields for quantities has been increased
  256  *   to advoid decimal overflow
  257  *
  258  * - table ORGAN is replaced by G_T_ORGAN
  259  *   - it is filled by the following ways :
  260  *     - at process time at selection screen if the
  261  *       user wants the selection via cc or plant
  262  *     - otherwise after the database selection of the stock
  263  *       tables
  264  *   - it contains less data fields
  265  *   - it contains all entries twice, for binary search
  266  *     with plant or valuation area
  267  *
  268  * - selection of databases MKPF and MSEG in one SELECT
  269  *   command with an inner JOIN
  270  *
  271  * - authority checks after the database selections
  272  *
  273  * - result of database selection from the both database tables
  274  *   MSEG and MKPF in working table G_F_MSEG_LEAN instead of
  275  *   the tables IMSEG and IMKPF
  276  *
  277  * - the number of processed data fields was reduced
  278  * - the user has the possibility to increase the number of
  279  *   the processed fields deleting the '*' in the types-command
  280  *   in include report RM07MLBD_CUST_FIELDS
  281  *
  282  * - the creation of the field catalog for the ALV considers
  283  *   only the fields of structure G_S_MSEG_LEAN
  284  *
  285  * - the new table G_T_BELEG contains the results for the ALV.
  286  *   the number of fields of table G_T_BELEG corresponds with
  287  *   the number of fields of table G_T_MSEG_LEAN.
  288  *
  289  * - the functions "define breakdown" and "choose" are inactivated
  290  *   in the menue, because they are are not carried out correctly
  291  *   in all blocks of the list

  293  ************************************************************************
  294  *     Anzeige der Materialbestände in einem Zeitintervall              *
  295  ************************************************************************
  296  *  Der Report gliedert sich im wesentlichen in folgende Verarbeitungs- *
  297  *  blöcke:                                                             *
  298  *  1) Definition des Einstiegsbildes und Vorbelegung einzelner         *
  299  *     Selektionsfelder, sowie Prüfung der eingegebenen Selektions-     *
  300  *     parameter und Berechtigungsprüfung                               *
  301  *  2) Lesen der aktuellen Bestandswerte                                *
  302  *  3) Lesen und Verarbeiten der Materialbelege                         *
  303  *  4) Berechnung der Bestandswerte zu den vorgegebenen Datümern        *
  304  *  5) Ausgabe der Bestände und Materialbelege                          *
  305  ************************************************************************

  376  *-----------------------------------------------------------"n571473
  377  * define the selection screen here                          "n571473
  378  *-----------------------------------------------------------"n571473
  379  SELECTION-SCREEN BEGIN OF BLOCK database-selection
  380    WITH FRAME TITLE TEXT-001.
  381  *  Text-001: Datenbankabgrenzungen
  382    SELECT-OPTIONS: matnr FOR mard-matnr MEMORY ID mat
  383                                         MATCHCODE OBJECT mat1.
  384  *ENHANCEMENT-POINT RM07MLBD_01 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD STATIC.
  385    SELECT-OPTIONS:
  386                    bukrs FOR t001-bukrs  MEMORY ID buk,
  387                    hkont FOR bseg-hkont  MODIF  ID hkt,
  388                    werks FOR t001w-werks MEMORY ID wrk,
  389                    lgort FOR t001l-lgort,
  390                    charg FOR mchb-charg,
  391                    bwtar FOR mbew-bwtar,
  392                    bwart FOR mseg-bwart.
  393    PARAMETERS sobkz LIKE mseg-sobkz.
  394    SELECTION-SCREEN SKIP.
  395    SELECT-OPTIONS: datum FOR mkpf-budat NO-EXTENSION.
  396  *  Datumsintervall für Selektion
  397  SELECTION-SCREEN END OF BLOCK database-selection.
  398
  399  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  400

  402    WITH FRAME TITLE TEXT-002.

  406      PARAMETERS lgbst LIKE am07m-lgbst RADIOBUTTON GROUP bart DEFAULT 'X'

 1922  FORM create_table_totals_hq.
 1923
 1924  * create output table
 1925    LOOP AT bestand.
 1926  *   part 1 : create header table g_t_totals_header
 1927      MOVE-CORRESPONDING  bestand        TO  g_s_totals_header.
 1928      MOVE  sobkz                        TO  g_s_totals_header-sobkz.
 1929
 1930      PERFORM  f2100_mat_text  USING  bestand-matnr.
 1931      MOVE  g_s_makt-maktx     TO  g_s_totals_header-maktx.
 1932
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     34
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1933      IF  bwbst IS INITIAL.
 1934  *     mode : stocks or special stocks
 1935        PERFORM  f2200_read_t001 USING bestand-werks.
 1936
 1937        MOVE  t001w-name1      TO  g_s_totals_header-name1.
 1938      ELSE.
 1939  *     mode : valuated stocks
 1940        IF  curm = '3'.
 1941  *       valuation level is company code
 1942          SELECT SINGLE butxt  FROM t001
 1943                               INTO g_f_butxt
 1944            WHERE  bukrs = bestand-bwkey.
 1945
 1946          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1947            MOVE  g_f_butxt    TO  g_s_totals_header-name1.
 1948          ELSE.
 1949            CLEAR              g_s_totals_header-name1.
 1950          ENDIF.
 1951        ELSE.
 1952  *       valuation level is plant -> take the name of the plant
 1953          PERFORM  f2200_read_t001 USING bestand-werks.
 1954
 1955          MOVE  t001w-name1    TO  g_s_totals_header-name1.
 1956        ENDIF.
 1957      ENDIF.
 1958
 1959      APPEND  g_s_totals_header     TO  g_t_totals_header.
 1960
 1961  *   part 2 : create 4 lines in item table g_t_totals_item
 1962      CLEAR                         g_s_totals_item.
 1963      MOVE : bestand-bwkey          TO  g_s_totals_item-bwkey,
 1964             bestand-werks          TO  g_s_totals_item-werks,
 1965             bestand-matnr          TO  g_s_totals_item-matnr,
 1966             bestand-charg          TO  g_s_totals_item-charg,
 1967             bestand-meins          TO  g_s_totals_item-meins,
 1968             bestand-waers          TO  g_s_totals_item-waers.
 1969
 1970  *   line with the stock on start date
 1971      MOVE : g_date_line_from       TO  g_s_totals_item-stock_type,
 1972             bestand-anfmenge       TO  g_s_totals_item-menge,
 1973             bestand-anfwert        TO  g_s_totals_item-wert.
 1974  *    ENHANCEMENT-POINT EHP605_RM07MLBD_08 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD .
 1975      PERFORM                       create_table_totals_hq_1.
 1976
 1977  *   line with the good receipts
 1978      IF  bwbst = 'X'.
 1979        MOVE : TEXT-030             TO  g_s_totals_item-stock_type+2,
 1980               bestand-soll         TO  g_s_totals_item-menge,
 1981               bestand-sollwert     TO  g_s_totals_item-wert.
 1982      ELSE.
 1983        MOVE : TEXT-005             TO  g_s_totals_item-stock_type+2,
 1984               bestand-soll         TO  g_s_totals_item-menge.
 1985      ENDIF.
 1986  *    ENHANCEMENT-POINT EHP605_RM07MLBD_09 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD .
 1987      PERFORM                       create_table_totals_hq_1.
 1988
 1989  *   line with the good issues
 1990      IF  bwbst = 'X'.
 1991        MOVE : TEXT-031             TO  g_s_totals_item-stock_type+2.
 1992        g_s_totals_item-menge       = bestand-haben      * -1.
 1993        g_s_totals_item-wert        = bestand-habenwert  * -1.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     35

 1994      ELSE.
 1995        MOVE : TEXT-006             TO  g_s_totals_item-stock_type+2.
 1996        g_s_totals_item-menge       = bestand-haben      * -1.
 1997      ENDIF.
 1998  *    ENHANCEMENT-POINT EHP605_RM07MLBD_10 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD .
 1999      PERFORM                       create_table_totals_hq_1.
 2000
 2001  *   line with the tock on end date

 2806
 2807  *  Aus dieser Unterroutine heraus werden implizit, d.h. in der Schleife
 2808  *  über alle selektierten Bestände, die zugehörigen Materialbelege
 2809  *  aufgerufen.
 2810  *  Die Bestände werden zum Anfangs- und Enddatum als Summen zu
 2811  *  folgendem Schlüssel, der im Listkopf geführt wird, ausgegeben:
 2812  *  Buchungskreis bzw. Werk, Material, Charge. Nicht-chargenpflichtige
 2813  *  Materialien werden auf Materialebene angezeigt.
 2814  *  Es folgt jeweils eine Liste mit den einzelnen Belegpositionen.
 2815
 2816  *********************** Ende HAUPTPROGRAMM *****************************

 2818  ************************* FORMROUTINEN *********************************

 2836  *  Die Selektionseingaben Buchungskreis und Werk werden hierarchisch
 2837  *  verstanden, d.h. es werden nur Werke innerhalb der angegebenen
 2838  *  Buchungskreise selektiert.
 2839  *  Lagerort-/Chargenbestand: Da die Werksbezeichnung eindeutig ist,
 2840  *  finden alle Selektionen auf Werksebene bzw. - falls mindestens ein
 2841  *  Lagerort eingegeben wurde - auf der Ebene der eingegebenen Lagerorte
 2842  *  statt. Die Ausgabe erfolgt auf Werksebene des Materials / der Charge.
 2843  *  Bewerteter Bestand: Die Ausgabe erfolgt auf Bewertungskreisebene,
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     49
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2844  *  d.h. je nach Einstellung in der Tabelle TCURM auf Werks- oder
 2845  *  Buchungskreisebene.
 2846
 2847  *  Feststellen, ob der Bewertungskreis auf Buchungskreis- oder
 2848  *  Werksebene liegt:
 2849  *  tcurm-bwkrs_cus = 1  =>  Bewertungskreis auf Werksebene,
 2850  *  tcurm-bwkrs_cus = 3  =>  Bewertungskreis auf Buchungskreisebene.
 2851    SELECT bwkrs_cus FROM tcurm INTO curm
 2852              ORDER BY PRIMARY KEY.
 2853    ENDSELECT.

 3355  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3356  *&      Form  SUMMEN_BILDEN
 3357  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3358  *       Bestandssummen zur Berechnung der Bestände                     *
 3359  *       zu 'datum-low' und 'datum-high'                                *
 3360  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3361  FORM summen_bilden.
 3362  * Some explanatory words on the strategy of material
 3363  * counting/valuation:
 3364  * ======================================================
 3365  * 1) Stock overview (no valuation):
 3366  *    The material document is accepted, if is has not been created
 3367  *    automatically or if it is not related to movements out of
 3368  *    the stock. For example, if a stock transfer is posted, the
 3369  *    system creates a material document with two lines: Out of
 3370  *    the old stock (accepted) and into the transfer stock (rejected,
 3371  *    because the material is not yet visible in the target location).
 3372  *    When the movement into the stock is posted, this is accepted.
 3373  * 2) Valuated stock:
 3374  *    a) Movements within a single plant (MA05, MA06 =
 3375  *       movement types 313-316) are ignored.
 3376  *    b) The moving of material out of a plant (303/304)
 3377  *       is counted and valuated in the emitting plant and
 3378  *       the target plant. The moving in
 3379  *       (305/306) is ignored, because
 3380  *       the valuated stock appears in the target at the
 3381  *       very moment of leaving the emitter.
 3382  *    c) Material documents without valuation string are ignored.
 3383  *------------- Summen von 'datum-high' bis Gegenwart ------------------*
 3384  * Performance Optimization:                                 "1784986
 3385  * Form is called from FORM create_table_for_detail!         "1784986

 3754  FORM f0400_create_fieldcat.
 3755
 3756    CLEAR                      g_s_fieldcat.
 3757
 3758  * lagerort                   storage location
 3759  * the following special stocks O, V, W need no storage location
 3760    IF  sobkz = 'O'  OR
 3761        sobkz = 'V'  OR
 3762        sobkz = 'W'.
 3763    ELSE.
 3764      g_s_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'LGORT'.
 3765      g_s_fieldcat-ref_tabname   = 'MSEG'.
 3766      g_s_fieldcat-sp_group      = 'O'.
 3767      PERFORM  f0410_fieldcat    USING  c_take   c_out.
 3768    ENDIF.
 3769
 3770  * Bewegungsart               movement type
 3771    g_s_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BWART'.
 3772    g_s_fieldcat-ref_tabname   = 'MSEG'.
 3773    g_s_fieldcat-sp_group      = 'M'.
 3774    PERFORM  f0410_fieldcat    USING  c_take   c_out.

 4402  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 4403  *    F0410_FIELDCAT
 4404  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 4405
 4406  FORM f0410_fieldcat
 4407           USING  l_f_check
 4408                  l_f_no_out   TYPE      slis_fieldcat_main-no_out.
 4409
 4410    DATA : l_f_continue(01) TYPE c,
 4411           l_f_type(01)     TYPE c,
 4412           l_f_fieldname    TYPE      stype_fields.
 4413
 4414    FIELD-SYMBOLS : <l_fs>.
 4415
 4416    IF  l_f_check = c_take.
 4417  *   take this entry without check
 4418      MOVE  'X'                TO  l_f_continue.
 4419    ELSE.
 4420  *   create key and look for fieldname
 4421      CONCATENATE              g_s_fieldcat-ref_tabname
 4422                               '~'
 4423                               g_s_fieldcat-fieldname
 4424                               INTO l_f_fieldname.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     75
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4425
 4426      READ TABLE g_t_mseg_fields         INTO g_s_mseg_fields
 4427                               WITH KEY
 4428                               fieldname = l_f_fieldname
 4429                               BINARY SEARCH.
 4430
 4431      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 4432        MOVE  'X'              TO  l_f_continue.
 4433      ELSE.
 4434  *     additional fields are displayed in wrong format :     "n480130
 4435  *     clear the working area for the field catalog when     "n480130
 4436  *     the current field should not be processed             "n480130
 4437        CLEAR                  g_s_fieldcat.                  "n480130
 4438        CLEAR                  l_f_continue.
 4439      ENDIF.
 4440    ENDIF.
 4441
 4442  * append entry to field catalog if field is in structure
 4443  * else leave this routine
 4444    IF l_f_continue IS INITIAL.
 4445      CLEAR                    g_s_fieldcat.
 4446      EXIT.
 4447    ENDIF.
 4448
 4449    IF  NOT l_f_no_out IS INITIAL.
 4450      MOVE  l_f_no_out         TO  g_s_fieldcat-no_out.
 4451    ENDIF.
 4452
 4453    ADD  : 1                   TO  g_f_col_pos.
 4454    MOVE : g_f_col_pos         TO  g_s_fieldcat-col_pos,
 4455           g_f_tabname         TO  g_s_fieldcat-tabname.
 4456    APPEND g_s_fieldcat        TO  fieldcat.
 4457
 4458  * create the table with the fields who will be enriched with colors
 4459  * and sign
 4460    IF  g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'MENGE'  OR
 4461        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'MEINS'  OR
 4462        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'DMBTR'  OR "Comment by Rahul Vasita on
        15.12.2023 18:29:04
 4463        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'WAERS'  OR
 4464        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'ERFMG'  OR
 4465        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'ERFME'  OR
 4466
 4467        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'BPMNG'  OR
 4468        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'BPRME'  OR
 4469        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'BSTMG'  OR
 4470        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'BSTME'  OR
 4471        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'EXBWR'  OR
 4472        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'VKWRT'  OR
 4473
 4474        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'EXVKW'  OR
 4475        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'VKWRA'  OR
 4476        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'LSMNG'  OR
 4477        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'LSMEH'  OR
 4478        g_s_fieldcat-fieldname  =  'SHKZG'.
 4479
 4480  *   look for the type of this field
 4481      CONCATENATE              g_s_fieldcat-ref_tabname
 4482                               '-'
 4483                               g_s_fieldcat-fieldname
 4484                               INTO l_f_fieldname.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     76
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4485
 4486      ASSIGN  (l_f_fieldname)  TO <l_fs>.
 4487
 4488      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 4489        DESCRIBE FIELD <l_fs>    TYPE  l_f_type.
 4490        MOVE : g_s_fieldcat-fieldname
 4491                               TO  g_t_color_fields-fieldname,
 4492             l_f_type          TO  g_t_color_fields-type.
 4493        APPEND                 g_t_color_fields.
 4494      ENDIF.
 4495    ENDIF.
 4496
 4497  *  ENHANCEMENT-POINT EHP605_RM07MLBD_16 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD .
 4498
 4499    CLEAR                      g_s_fieldcat.
 4500
 4501  ENDFORM.                     "F0410_FIELDCAT

 4551                               USING KEY (lv_bsim_key). "#EC CI_NOORDER "2
       936002
 4552                                                              "n443935
 4553        IF  g_s_bsim_lean-bukrs  =  g_s_mseg_old-bukrs  AND   "n443935

 4555            g_s_bsim_lean-matnr  =  g_s_mseg_old-matnr  AND   "n443935
 4556            g_s_bsim_lean-bwtar  =  g_s_mseg_old-bwtar  AND   "n443935

 4559            g_s_bsim_lean-budat  =  g_s_mseg_old-budat  AND   "n443935
 4560  *         IS-OIL posts GR for PO with "MB11" which leads to    "2575057

 4564            g_s_bsim_lean-blart(1) = g_s_mseg_old-blart(1) AND "2707850
 4565            g_s_bsim_lean-awkey  =  matkey.                   "n443935

 4574            EXIT.                                             "n443935
 4575          ENDIF.                                              "2575057
 4576        ENDIF.                                                "n443935

 4877  FORM user_command                                           "#EC CALLED
 4878                     USING     r_ucomm      LIKE  sy-ucomm
 4879                               rs_selfield  TYPE  slis_selfield.
 4880
 4881    TYPES: BEGIN OF ty_s_sel,
 4882             mblnr LIKE  mseg-mblnr,

 4892    DATA: l_value(10) TYPE c,                                 "n1583816
 4893          ls_sel      TYPE ty_s_sel,
 4894          lt_sel      TYPE ty_t_sel,
 4895          l_lines     LIKE sy-tabix,
 4896          ls_fc       TYPE slis_fieldcat_alv,
 4897          lt_fc       TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv,
 4898          ls_selfield TYPE slis_selfield,
 4899          l_fi_doc    TYPE c  LENGTH 1.                       "n1511550
 4900
 4901  * Unfortunately the output list of this report consists
 4902  * of several ALVs, one started at the end-event of the other.
 4903  * This abstrucse programming style was chosen to create a list
 4904  * layout similar to the one in release 3.1. Now this causes a severe
 4905  * problem: When selecting a line, we do not know which ALV (and there-
 4906  * for which line in table IMSEG) has been selected. We can only use
 4907  * the value of the selected field to access the data-table.
 4908  * In case of ambiguities, a popup has to be transmitted where the
 4909  * user has to reselect the document he wants to see. This is
 4910  * difficult to understand, if you do not know the problems of
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     83
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4911  * programming ABAP.
 4912
 4913    "Comment Start by Rahul Vasita on 18.12.2023 16:01:00
 4914    CASE r_ucomm.
 4915      WHEN '9PBP'.
 4916  *     Get line of IMSEG which "look" like the one selected
 4917        l_value = rs_selfield-value.
 4918        CHECK NOT l_value IS INITIAL.                         "204872
 4919        CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'              "n1604106
 4920          EXPORTING                                              "n1604106
 4921            input  = l_value                                     "n1604106
 4922          IMPORTING                                              "n1604106
 4923            output = l_value.                                    "n1604106
 4924        IF rs_selfield-sel_tab_field = 'G_T_BELEGE-MBLNR' OR
 4925           rs_selfield-sel_tab_field = 'G_T_BELEGE1-MBLNR'.
 4926          LOOP AT g_t_mseg_lean          INTO  g_s_mseg_lean
 4927                                         WHERE mblnr = l_value.
 4928            ls_sel-mblnr = g_s_mseg_lean-mblnr.
 4929            ls_sel-mjahr = g_s_mseg_lean-mjahr.
 4930            ls_sel-zeile = g_s_mseg_lean-zeile.
 4931            COLLECT ls_sel INTO lt_sel.
 4932          ENDLOOP.
 4933
 4934        ELSEIF rs_selfield-sel_tab_field = 'G_T_BELEGE-BELNR' OR
 4935               rs_selfield-sel_tab_field = 'G_T_BELEGE1-BELNR'.
 4936          l_fi_doc = 'X'.
 4937          LOOP AT g_t_mseg_lean          INTO  g_s_mseg_lean
 4938                                         WHERE belnr = l_value.
 4939            ls_sel-belnr = g_s_mseg_lean-belnr.
 4940            ls_sel-gjahr = g_s_mseg_lean-gjahr.
 4941            ls_sel-bukrs = g_s_mseg_lean-bukrs.
 4942            COLLECT ls_sel INTO lt_sel.
 4943          ENDLOOP.
 4944
 4945        ENDIF.
 4946        SORT lt_sel BY mjahr mblnr zeile bukrs belnr gjahr.
 4947  *     Read first line. If L_LINES = 1, LS_SEL is filled properly.
 4948        READ TABLE lt_sel INTO ls_sel INDEX 1.
 4949        DESCRIBE TABLE lt_sel LINES l_lines.
 4950  *     If no line found, the cursor was not on a useful value.
 4951        IF l_lines = 0.
 4952          MESSAGE s270.
 4953          EXIT.
 4954        ENDIF.
 4955  *     If more than one line found, it gets difficult. We send a popup
 4956  *     where the user may select a single line.
 4957        IF l_lines > 1.
 4958  *       Create fieldcatalog
 4959          DEFINE fc_add.
 4960            ls_fc-fieldname     = &1.
 4961            ls_fc-ref_tabname   = &2.
 4962            ls_fc-ref_fieldname = &3.
 4963            APPEND ls_fc TO lt_fc.
 4964          END-OF-DEFINITION.
 4965          CLEAR ls_sel.
 4966          IF l_fi_doc IS INITIAL.
 4967            fc_add 'MBLNR' 'MKPF' 'MBLNR'.
 4968            fc_add 'MJAHR' 'MKPF' 'MJAHR'.
 4969            fc_add 'ZEILE' 'MSEG' 'ZEILE'.
 4970          ELSE.
 4971            fc_add 'BUKRS' 'BKPF' 'BUKRS'.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     84
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 4972            fc_add 'BELNR' 'BKPF' 'BELNR'.
 4973            fc_add 'GJAHR' 'BKPF' 'GJAHR'.
 4974          ENDIF.
 4975
 4976          CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_POPUP_TO_SELECT'
 4977            EXPORTING
 4978              i_zebra     = 'X'

 5245  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 5246  *  calculate_offsets.
 5247  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 5248
 5249  * calculate the offsets for the list header
 5250
 5251  FORM calculate_offsets.
 5252
 5253  *  working area
 5254    DATA : l_text(132)         TYPE c.
 5255
 5256  * get the maximal length of the text elements to be used
 5257    PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  TEXT-020.
 5258    PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  TEXT-021.
 5259    PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  TEXT-022.
 5260    PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  TEXT-023.
 5261    PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  TEXT-025.
 5262
 5263    g_offset_header            =  g_f_length_max + 3.
 5264
 5265    CLEAR                      g_f_length_max.
 5266
 5267    IF  bwbst IS INITIAL.
 5268  *     stocks and quantities only
 5269      MOVE   TEXT-007          TO  g_date_line_from-text.
 5270      WRITE : datum-low        TO  g_date_line_from-datum DD/MM/YYYY.
 5271      CONDENSE                 g_date_line_from.
 5272      PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  g_date_line_from.
 5273
 5274      MOVE  TEXT-005           TO  g_text_line-text.
 5275      PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  g_text_line.
 5276
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Program ZMM_RM07MLBD                          Page     89
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 5277      MOVE  TEXT-006           TO  g_text_line.
 5278      PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  g_text_line.
 5279
 5280      MOVE   TEXT-007          TO  g_date_line_to-text.
 5281      WRITE : datum-high       TO  g_date_line_to-datum DD/MM/YYYY.
 5282      CONDENSE                 g_date_line_to.
 5283    ELSE.
 5284  *     stocks, quantities, and values
 5285      MOVE   TEXT-008          TO  g_date_line_from-text.
 5286      WRITE : datum-low        TO  g_date_line_from-datum DD/MM/YYYY.
 5287      CONDENSE                 g_date_line_from.
 5288
 5289  *     the start and end dates were shown incorrectly in the "n856424
 5290  *     headlines in the mode valuated stock                  "n856424
 5291      PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  g_date_line_from.   "n856424
 5292
 5293      MOVE  TEXT-030           TO  g_text_line-text.
 5294      PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  g_text_line.
 5295
 5296      MOVE  TEXT-031           TO  g_text_line-text.
 5297      PERFORM  get_max_text_length USING  g_text_line.
 5298
 5299      MOVE   TEXT-008          TO  g_date_line_to-text.
 5300      WRITE : datum-high       TO  g_date_line_to-datum DD/MM/YYYY.
 5301      CONDENSE                 g_date_line_to.
 5302    ENDIF.
 5303
 5304  * calculate the offsets for the following columns
 5305    g_offset_qty               =  g_f_length_max +  2.
 5306    g_offset_unit              =  g_offset_qty   + 25.
 5307    g_offset_value             =  g_offset_unit  +  8.
 5308    g_offset_curr              =  g_offset_value + 25.
 5309
 5310  *  ENHANCEMENT-POINT EHP605_RM07MLBD_19 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD .
 5311
 5312  ENDFORM.                     " calculate_offsets.

   18  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*

   20  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
   21
   22  * This include contains the structure for additional fields for report
   23  * RM07MLBD ( transaction MB5B )
   24
   25  * the following fields are not shown in the list of report
   26  * RM07MLBD
   27  * If you want to insert some of these fields in the list of the
   28  * hidden fields delete the '*' in the type definition.
   29  *
   30  * There are only fields from database table MSEG possible
   31  * Please use only the following fields, because these fields
   32  * are considered during the creation of the field catalog;
   33  * plaese consider, that each active field will cost performance
   34
   35  ENHANCEMENT-SECTION RM07MLBD_CUST_FIELDS_01 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD_CUST_FIELD
       S STATIC .
   36  TYPES : BEGIN OF STYPE_MB5B_ADD,
   37            DUMMY(01)          TYPE C,             "filler
   38  *
   39  * the following fields of database table MSEG can be activated
   40  *         insmk    like      mseg-insmk,    "Bestandsart
   41  *         LIFNR    LIKE      MSEG-LIFNR,    "Kontonummer Lieferant

  274  *             transaction key ( KTOSL ) 'BSX'               "n497992
  275  *             this mode will cost performance               "n497992

   47  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
   48  *&      Form  INITIALISIERUNG
   49  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
   50  *       Vorbelegung der Anzeigevariante                                *
   51  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
   52
   53  FORM initialisierung.
   54
   55    repid = sy-repid.
   56    variant_save = 'A'.
   57    CLEAR variante.
   58    variante-report = repid.
   59  * Default-Variante holen:
   60    def_variante = variante.
   61
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    108
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

   62    CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_VARIANT_DEFAULT_GET'
   63      EXPORTING
   64        i_save     = variant_save
   65      CHANGING
   66        cs_variant = def_variante
   67      EXCEPTIONS
   68        not_found  = 2.
   69    IF sy-subrc = 0.
   70  *   save the initial, e.g. default variant                  "n599218
   71      MOVE  def_variante-variant  TO  alv_default_variant.    "n599218
   72      p_vari = def_variante-variant.
   73    ENDIF.
   74  *  print-no_print_listinfos = 'X'.
   75
   76  * show the block with the parameters for the best database  "n921165
   77  * access depending on the database system                   "n921165
   78    MOVE  sy-dbsys+0(03)       TO  g_f_database.              "n921165
   79                                                              "n921165
   80    BREAK-POINT                ID mmim_rep_mb5b.              "n921164
   81  * dynamic break-point : check installed database system     "n921165
   82                                                              "n921165
   83    IF  g_f_database = 'INF'   OR                             "n921165
   84        g_f_database = 'DB6'   OR                             "n921165
   85        g_f_database = 'MSS'   OR   " consider MSSQL, too     "n921165
   86        g_f_database = 'ADA'.                                 "n921165
   87  *   show the 3 parameters                                   "n921165
   88      MOVE : 'X'               TO  g_flag_db_parameters.      "n921165
   89    ELSE.                                                     "n921165
   90  *   ORACLE and DB2 work hist histograms                     "n921165
   91  *   do not show the 3 parameters on the selection screen    "n921165
   92      CLEAR                    g_flag_db_parameters.          "n921165
   93  *   set hidden parameters                                   "n921165
   94      MOVE  'X'                TO  pa_dbstd.                  "n921165
   95      CLEAR :                  pa_dbmat, pa_dbdat.            "n921165
   96    ENDIF.                                                    "n921165
   97
   98    CALL FUNCTION 'MB_CHECK_MSEG_CONVERSION_DONE'             "n1558298
   99      IMPORTING                                               "n1558298
  100        e_conversion_done = g_f_msegex_act.             "n1558298
  101
  102  ENDFORM.                               " INITIALISIERUNG

  104  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  105  *&      Form  AKTUELLE_BESTAENDE
  106  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  107  *      Ermittlung der aktuellen eigenen Bestände,
  108  *      d.h. der bewerteten Bestände und des Retourensperrbestandes,
  109  *      auf Lagerortebene und auf Material- bzw. Chargenebene;

  828  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  829  *&      Form  TABELLEN_LESEN
  830  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    122
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  831  *       Lesen der Materialkurztexte (Tabelle MAKT),                    *
  832  *       der Mengeneinheiten (Tabelle MARA) und                         *
  833  *       Mengen- und Wertfortschreibung zum Material (Tabelle T134M)    *
  834  *       (Letzteres ist zum Aussortieren der unbewerteten bzw.          *
  835  *       kontierten Warenbewegungen notwendig)                          *
  836  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  837
  838  FORM tabellen_lesen.
  839
  840    IF  NOT g_t_mat_key[] IS INITIAL.                         "n451923
  841      ENHANCEMENT-SECTION EHP605_TABELLEN_LESEN_01 SPOTS ES_RM07MLBD .
  842  *   select the material masters
  843      PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'MARA' ''.                "n1710850
  844      SELECT matnr meins mtart FROM mara  CONNECTION (dbcon)  "n1710850
  845                     INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE imara
  846                     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN g_t_mat_key
  847                               WHERE  matnr  =  g_t_mat_key-matnr.
  848      END-ENHANCEMENT-SECTION.
  849
  850  *   select the short text for all materials
  851  *   take only the necessary fields                          "n451923
  852      PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'MAKT' ''.                "n1710850
  853      SELECT matnr maktx       FROM makt CONNECTION (dbcon)   "n1710850
  854           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE g_t_makt        "n451923
  855                     FOR ALL ENTRIES IN g_t_mat_key
  856                     WHERE  matnr = g_t_mat_key-matnr
  857                       AND  spras = sy-langu.
  858
  859      SORT  imara              BY  matnr.                     "n451923
  860      SORT  g_t_makt           BY  matnr.                     "n451923
  861      FREE                     g_t_mat_key.
  862    ENDIF.
  863
  864    DATA: BEGIN OF k1 OCCURS 0,
  865      mtart LIKE t134m-mtart,
  866    END OF k1.
  867    REFRESH k1.
  868
  869    LOOP AT imara.
  870      k1-mtart = imara-mtart.
  871      COLLECT k1.
  872    ENDLOOP.
  873
  874    IF  NOT k1[] IS INITIAL.                                  "n451923
  875      SELECT * FROM t134m                                 "#EC CI_GENBUFF
  876             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it134m
  877             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN k1         WHERE mtart = k1-mtart
  878                                           AND   bwkey IN g_ra_bwkey.
  879    ENDIF.                                                    "n451923
  880
  881    LOOP AT it134m.
  882  *   read table organ with key bwkey = it134m-bwkey.
  883      PERFORM  f9300_read_organ
  884                     USING     c_bwkey     it134m-bwkey.
  885
  886      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
  887        DELETE it134m.
  888      ENDIF.
  889    ENDLOOP.
  890
  891  * To find postings with valuation string, but without relevance for
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    123
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  892  * the valuated stock, Big-G recommended this logic:
  893  * Take lines from MSEG where for the combination BUSTW/XAUTO=XBGBB
  894  * there is an entry in T156W with key BSX.
  895    SELECT bustw xbgbb FROM t156w
  896                       INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it156w
  897                       WHERE vorsl = 'BSX'.
  898    SORT it156w BY bustw xbgbb.
  899    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it156w.
  900    DELETE it156w WHERE bustw = space.
  901
  902  ENDFORM.                               " TABELLEN_LESEN

  945  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

  947  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  948  *  Beim Erfassen der Werte ist es notwendig, die Buchhaltungsbelege    *
  949  *  zum Material zu lesen, um abweichende Werte zwischen Wareneingang   *
  950  *  und Rechnungseingang sowie Nachbelastungen zu berücksichtigen.      *
  951  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  952
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    124
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  953  FORM fi_belege_lesen.
  954
  955  * Not related to note 184465, but a significant performance issue
  956  * if ORGAN is large due to many plants/storage locations.
  957    DATA: BEGIN OF t_bwkey OCCURS 0,                          "184465
  958            bwkey LIKE bsim-bwkey,                            "184465
  959          END OF t_bwkey.                                     "184465
  960
  961    LOOP AT g_t_organ          WHERE  keytype  =  c_bwkey.
  962      MOVE g_t_organ-bwkey     TO  t_bwkey-bwkey.
  963      COLLECT t_bwkey.                                        "184465
  964    ENDLOOP.                                                  "184465
  965
  966    READ TABLE t_bwkey INDEX 1.                               "184465
  967    CHECK sy-subrc = 0.                                       "184465
  968
  969    PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'BSIM' ''.                  "n1710850
  970    SELECT * FROM bsim  CONNECTION (dbcon)                    "n1710850
  971           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE g_t_bsim_lean   "n443935
  972             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN t_bwkey   WHERE  bwkey = t_bwkey-bwkey
  973                                          AND    matnr IN matnr
  974                                          AND    bwtar IN bwtar
  975                                          AND    budat >= datum-low.
  976
  977    LOOP AT g_t_bsim_lean      INTO  g_s_bsim_lean.           "n443935
  978      PERFORM  f9300_read_organ
  979                     USING     c_bwkey  g_s_bsim_lean-bwkey.  "n443935
  980
  981      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
  982  *     record found : the user has the authority, go on
  983        MOVE  g_s_organ-bukrs  TO  g_s_bsim_lean-bukrs.       "n443935
  984        MODIFY  g_t_bsim_lean  FROM  g_s_bsim_lean            "n443935
  985                               TRANSPORTING  bukrs.           "n451923
  986
  987  *     create working table with the keys for the FI documents
  988        MOVE-CORRESPONDING  g_s_bsim_lean                     "n443935
  989                               TO  g_t_bkpf_key.              "n443935
  990        APPEND                 g_t_bkpf_key.
  991      ELSE.
  992        DELETE                 g_t_bsim_lean.                 "n443935
  993      ENDIF.
  994    ENDLOOP.
  995

  998  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
  999  *&      Form  BELEGE_SORTIEREN
 1000  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1001  *    Die Materialbelege werden anhand des Buchungsdatums sortiert.
 1002  *    Die Materialbelege mit Buchungsdatum zwischen 'datum-high'
 1003  *    und dem aktuellen Datum werden in der internen Tabelle IMSWEG
 1004  *    gesammelt, während die Materialbelege mit Buchungsdatum
 1005  *    zwischen 'datum-low' und 'datum-high' in der internen Tabelle
 1006  *    IMSEG verbleiben.
 1007  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1008
 1009  FORM belege_sortieren.
 1010
 1011    aktdat = sy-datlo + 30.
 1012    IF NOT ( datum-high IS INITIAL OR datum-high > aktdat ).
 1013      LOOP AT g_t_mseg_lean    INTO  g_s_mseg_lean
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    125
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1014                               WHERE budat > datum-high.
 1015        MOVE-CORRESPONDING g_s_mseg_lean TO imsweg.
 1016        APPEND imsweg.
 1017        DELETE                 g_t_mseg_lean.
 1018      ENDLOOP.
 1019    ENDIF.
 1020
 1021    DESCRIBE TABLE imsweg LINES index_2.
 1022
 1023  ENDFORM.                               " BELEGE_SORTIEREN

 1025  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1026  *&      Form  KONTIERT_AUSSORTIEREN
 1027  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1028  *       Aussortierung der kontierten Belegpositionen,                  *
 1029  *       da diese Mengen nicht bestandsrelevant sind                    *
 1030  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1031
 1032  FORM kontiert_aussortieren.
 1033
 1034  * process table g_t_mseg_lean
 1035  * loop at imseg where kzvbr <> space and                         "144845
 1036  *     ( kzbew = 'B' or kzbew = 'F' ).                            "144845
 1037  *     read table imara with key matnr = imseg-matnr.
 1038  *     read table it134m with key mtart = imara-mtart.
 1039  *     if not it134m-mengu is initial and not it134m-wertu is initial.
 1040  *  Die Felder 'mengu' und 'wertu' (Mengen- bzw. Wertfortschreibung)
 1041  *  sind ab Release 3.0 D auch in die Tabelle MSEG aufgenommen.
 1042  *  Die Einträge in der Tabelle T134M stellen nach wie vor die generelle
 1043  *  Einstellung dar; auf Positionsebene sind jedoch Abänderungen möglich,
 1044  *  die anhand der Einträge in der Tabelle MSEG nachverfolgt werden
 1045  *  können.
 1046  *       delete imseg.
 1047  *     endif.
 1048  * endloop.
 1049
 1050    DATA : l_f_bwkey           LIKE  t001k-bwkey.             "n497992
 1051
 1052    SORT  it134m               BY  bwkey  mtart.              "n497992
 1053
 1054    LOOP AT g_t_mseg_lean      INTO  g_s_mseg_lean
 1055                               WHERE  kzvbr <> space
 1056                                 AND ( kzbew = 'B' OR kzbew = 'F' ).
 1057
 1058  *   get the valuation area                                  "n497992
 1059      IF  curm = '3'.                                         "n497992
 1060  *     valuation level is company code                       "n497992
 1061        IF  g_s_mseg_lean-bukrs IS INITIAL.                   "n497992
 1062  *       get the valuation area for this plant               "n497992
 1063          PERFORM  f9300_read_organ                           "n497992
 1064                     USING     c_werks   g_s_mseg_lean-werks. "n497992
 1065                                                              "n497992
 1066          MOVE  g_s_organ-bwkey     TO  l_f_bwkey.            "n497992
 1067        ELSE.                                                 "n497992
 1068          MOVE  g_s_mseg_lean-bukrs TO  l_f_bwkey.            "n497992
 1069        ENDIF.                                                "n497992
 1070      ELSE.                                                   "n497992
 1071  *     valuation level is plant                              "n497992
 1072        MOVE  g_s_mseg_lean-werks   TO  l_f_bwkey.            "n497992
 1073      ENDIF.                                                  "n497992
 1074
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    126
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1075      READ TABLE imara WITH KEY matnr = g_s_mseg_lean-matnr
 1076                               BINARY SEARCH.
 1077
 1078      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1079        READ TABLE it134m      WITH KEY  bwkey = l_f_bwkey    "n497992
 1080                                         mtart = imara-mtart  "n497992
 1081                               BINARY SEARCH.
 1082
 1083        IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1084          IF NOT it134m-mengu IS INITIAL AND
 1085             NOT it134m-wertu IS INITIAL.
 1086            IF LGBST = abap_true.                             "3164181
 1087              IF NOT ( g_s_mseg_lean-mengu = abap_true AND
 1088                       g_s_mseg_lean-wertu = abap_false ).
 1089                DELETE              g_t_mseg_lean.
 1090              ENDIF.
 1091            ELSE.
 1092              DELETE              g_t_mseg_lean.
 1093            ENDIF.
 1094          ENDIF.
 1095        ENDIF.
 1096      ELSE.
 1097        DELETE                  g_t_mseg_lean.
 1098      ENDIF.
 1099    ENDLOOP.
 1100
 1101  ENDFORM.                               " KONTIERT_AUSSORTIEREN

 1103  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1104  *&      Form  BELEGE_ERGAENZEN (engl. enrich documents)
 1105  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1106  * Material documents and FI documents from BSIM are merged together.
 1107  * Complications:
 1108  * - A material document can have more than one FI document.
 1109  * - There are FI documents without material documnts
 1110  * - There are material documents without FI documents
 1111  * - The document type is customizeable
 1112  * - There is no link from the materia document position to
 1113  *   the FI document entry in BSIM (except URZEILE, but this
 1114  *   can be filled incorrectly)

 2612  FORM create_headline.                                       "n599218
 2613                                                              "n599218
 2614    DATA : l_offset            TYPE i,                        "n599218
 2615           l_strlen            TYPE i.                        "n599218
 2616                                                              "n599218
 2617  * get the length of the title                               "n599218
 2618    COMPUTE  l_strlen          = strlen( sy-title ).          "n599218
 2619                                                              "n599218
 2620    IF  bwbst IS INITIAL.                                     "n599218
 2621  *   stocks only --> small line with 77 bytes                "n599218
 2622      IF      l_strlen   =  59.                               "n599218
 2623        MOVE : sy-title        TO  g_s_header_77-title.       "n599218
 2624      ELSEIF  l_strlen   >  59.                               "n599218
 2625        MOVE : sy-title        TO  g_s_header_77-title,       "n599218
 2626               '...'          TO  g_s_header_77-title+56(03). "n599218
 2627      ELSE.                                                   "n599218
 2628        COMPUTE  l_offset      =  ( 59 - l_strlen ) / 2.      "n599218
 2629        MOVE : sy-title     TO  g_s_header_77-title+l_offset. "n599218
 2630      ENDIF.                                                  "n599218
 2631                                                              "n599218
 2632      WRITE : sy-datlo DD/MM/YYYY   TO  g_s_header_77-date.   "n599218
 2633    ELSE.                                                     "n599218
 2634  *   stocks and values --> wide line with 91 bytes           "n599218
 2635      IF      l_strlen   =  73.                               "n599218
 2636        MOVE : sy-title        TO  g_s_header_91-title.       "n599218
 2637      ELSEIF  l_strlen   >  73.                               "n599218
 2638        MOVE : sy-title        TO  g_s_header_91-title,       "n599218
 2639               '...'          TO  g_s_header_91-title+70(03). "n599218
 2640      ELSE.                                                   "n599218
 2641        COMPUTE  l_offset      =  ( 73 - l_strlen ) / 2.      "n599218
 2642        MOVE : sy-title     TO  g_s_header_91-title+l_offset. "n599218
 2643      ENDIF.                                                  "n599218
 2644                                                              "n599218
 2645      WRITE : sy-datlo DD/MM/YYYY   TO  g_s_header_91-date.   "n599218
 2646    ENDIF.                                                    "n599218
 2647                                                              "n599218
 2648  * create the end lines, too                                 "n599218
 2649    CONCATENATE  text-062      "End of List                   "n599218
 2650                 ':'           sy-title                       "n599218
 2651                               INTO  g_end_line_77            "n599218
 2652                               SEPARATED BY space.            "n599218
 2653    MOVE : g_end_line_77       TO  g_end_line_91.             "n599218
 2654                                                              "n599218
 2655  ENDFORM.                     "create_headline               "n599218

 2666
 2667  FORM fill_data_table
 2668           TABLES    l_t_belege      TYPE  stab_belege
 2669           USING     l_matnr         TYPE  mseg-matnr
 2670                     l_werks         TYPE  mseg-werks
 2671                     l_charg         TYPE  mseg-charg.
 2672
 2673  * define local data fields
 2674    DATA : l_s_belege          TYPE  stype_belege.
 2675
 2676  * sort table with the MM docs only once
 2677    IF  g_flag_sorted  IS INITIAL.
 2678      MOVE  'X'                TO  g_flag_sorted.
 2679      SORT  g_t_mseg_lean      BY  matnr  werks  charg.
 2680    ENDIF.
 2681
 2682  * read the first matching line depending on the batch
 2683    IF  l_charg IS INITIAL.
 2684      READ  TABLE g_t_mseg_lean     ASSIGNING  <g_fs_mseg_lean>
 2685        WITH KEY matnr = l_matnr
 2686                 werks = l_werks    BINARY SEARCH.
 2687    ELSE.
 2688      READ  TABLE g_t_mseg_lean     ASSIGNING  <g_fs_mseg_lean>
 2689        WITH KEY matnr = l_matnr
 2690                 werks = l_werks
 2691                 charg = l_charg    BINARY SEARCH.
 2692    ENDIF.
 2693
 2694  * the first entry found ? -> go on
 2695    CHECK sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 2696
 2697    MOVE  sy-tabix             TO  g_tabix_set.
 2698
 2699  * go on with sequential reading
 2700    LOOP AT g_t_mseg_lean     INTO  g_s_mseg_lean
 2701      FROM g_tabix_set.
 2702
 2703  *   take this entry when the key fields match
 2704      IF  g_s_mseg_lean-matnr = l_matnr   AND
 2705          g_s_mseg_lean-werks = l_werks.
 2706
 2707  *     cnsider the batches when this report runs in mode
 2708  *     "storage loc/batches" or "special stock"
 2709        IF  bwbst IS INITIAL.
 2710          CHECK : xchar               IS INITIAL       OR
 2711                  g_s_mseg_lean-charg = l_charg.
 2712        ENDIF.
 2713
 2714        MOVE-CORRESPONDING g_s_mseg_lean
 2715                               TO  l_s_belege.
 2716
 2717  *     enrich some fields with color and numeric fields with sign
 2718  *     the negative sign was not set for GI postings         "n944522
 2719        PERFORM  f9500_set_color_and_sign                     "n944522
 2720                         USING  l_s_belege  'L_S_BELEGE'.     "n944522
 2721
 2722        APPEND  l_s_belege     TO  l_t_belege.
 2723      ELSE.
 2724        EXIT.
 2725      ENDIF.
 2726    ENDLOOP.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    154
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2727
 2728  ENDFORM.                    " fill_data_table

 2734  FORM process_plants_of_bwkey
 2735           TABLES    l2_t_belege  TYPE  stab_belege
 2736           USING     l2_matnr     TYPE  mseg-matnr
 2737                     l2_bwkey     TYPE  mbew-bwkey.
 2738

 2740    FIELD-SYMBOLS :
 2741      <l_fs_organ>             TYPE  stype_organ.
 2742    DATA : l_tabix_start       TYPE  sy-tabix.
 2743

 2745    IF  curm = '1'.
 2746  *   valuation level is plant / process the MM docs
 2747      PERFORM  fill_data_table TABLES    l2_t_belege          "n921165
 2748                               USING     l2_matnr             "n921165
 2749                                         l2_bwkey             "n921165
 2750                                         space.               "n921165
 2751      EXIT.                    " leave this routine
 2752    ENDIF.

 2754  * valuation leve = company code : plenty of plants could
 2755  * be assigned to the valuation area, look for the assigned
 2756  * plants and look for the MM doc per plant
 2757
 2758  * look for the first valuation area
 2759    READ TABLE g_t_organ       ASSIGNING  <l_fs_organ>
 2760      WITH KEY keytype  = c_bwkey
 2761               keyfield = l2_bwkey
 2762                               BINARY SEARCH.
 2763

 2765    CHECK sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 2766
 2767    MOVE  sy-tabix             TO  l_tabix_start.
 2768

 2770    LOOP AT g_t_organ          ASSIGNING <l_fs_organ>
 2771      FROM l_tabix_start.
 2772
 2773      IF  <l_fs_organ>-keytype   = c_bwkey      AND
 2774          <l_fs_organ>-keyfield  = l2_bwkey.
 2775  *     process the MM docs from this plant
 2776        PERFORM  fill_data_table                              "n921165
 2777                               TABLES    l2_t_belege          "n921165
 2778                               USING     l2_matnr             "n921165
 2779                                         <l_fs_organ>-werks   "n921165
 2780                                         space.               "n921165
 2781      ELSE.
 2782        EXIT.
 2783      ENDIF.
 2784    ENDLOOP.

 2926  **********************************************************************
 2927  * Read accounts and account determination rules for specified accounts a
       nd organizations
 2928    DATA:
 2929      lt_rules    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF t030r,
 2930      lt_accounts TYPE ltt_account.
 2931
 2932    SELECT
 2933      account~ktopl
 2934
 2935      account~bwmod
 2936      account~bklas
 2937
 2938      account~konts AS saknr
 2939
 2940    FROM
 2941      t030 AS account
 2942    INTO
 2943      CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE lt_accounts
 2944
 2945    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN
 2946      lt_organ
 2947    WHERE
 2948          account~ktopl = lt_organ-ktopl
 2949      and account~ktosl = 'BSX'
 2950      and account~komok = ''
 2951      and account~konts IN hkont.
 2952
 2953
 2954    SELECT *
 2955    FROM
 2956      t030r as standart_account_rule
 2957    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE
 2958      lt_rules
 2959    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN
 2960      lt_organ
 2961    WHERE
 2962          standart_account_rule~ktopl = lt_organ-ktopl
 2963      AND standart_account_rule~ktosl EQ 'BSX'
 2964    ORDER BY
 2965      PRIMARY KEY.
 2966
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    158
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2967
 2968  * determine if rules restrict selection by valuation grouping codes or v
       aluation classes
 2969    DATA:
 2970      lv_bklas_restricted TYPE abap_bool,
 2971      lv_bwmod_restricted TYPE abap_bool.
 2972    FIELD-SYMBOLS:
 2973      <fs_valuation_area>   TYPE lts_valuation_area,
 2974      <fs_selected_account> TYPE lts_account,
 2975      <fs_standart_account_rule> TYPE t030r.
 2976
 2977    READ TABLE lt_rules WITH KEY xbwmo = 'X' TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
 2978    IF sy-subrc = 0.
 2979      lv_bwmod_restricted = 'X'.
 2980    ELSE.
 2981      lv_bwmod_restricted = ''.
 2982    ENDIF.
 2983
 2984    READ TABLE lt_rules WITH KEY xbkla = 'X' TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS.
 2985    IF sy-subrc = 0.
 2986      lv_bklas_restricted = 'X'.
 2987    ELSE.
 2988      lv_bklas_restricted = ''.
 2989    ENDIF.
 2990
 2991    g_ra_bwkey-sign = 'I'.
 2992    g_ra_bwkey-option = 'EQ'.
 2993
 2994  * if selection is restricted by valuation grouping code(BWMOD) also rest
       rict valuation area selection
 2995    IF lv_bwmod_restricted = abap_true.
 2996      CLEAR g_ra_bwkey-low.
 2997      LOOP AT lt_rules ASSIGNING <fs_standart_account_rule> WHERE XBWMO =
       'X'.
 2998          LOOP AT lt_accounts ASSIGNING <fs_selected_account> WHERE ktopl
       = <fs_standart_account_rule>-ktopl.
 2999            LOOP AT lt_valuation_areas ASSIGNING <fs_valuation_area> WHERE
        bwmod = <fs_selected_account>-bwmod.
 3000              g_ra_bwkey-low = <fs_valuation_area>-bwkey.
 3001              APPEND g_ra_bwkey TO g_ra_bwkey.
 3002            ENDLOOP.
 3003          ENDLOOP.
 3004      ENDLOOP.
 3005    ENDIF.
 3006    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM g_ra_bwkey.
 3007
 3008
 3009    IF lt_accounts IS INITIAL.
 3010      MESSAGE s289.
 3011  *   no data contained in the selection
 3012      PERFORM anforderungsbild.
 3013    ENDIF.

 3250  *&      Form  GET_ACC_DET
 3251  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3252  *       Get G/L account from current account determination settings
 3253  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3254  FORM get_acc_det  CHANGING cs_accdet  TYPE stype_accdet.
 3255
 3256    TYPES:
 3257      BEGIN OF ltt_acc,
 3258        bklas   LIKE t030-bklas,
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    163
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3259        bwmod   LIKE t030-bwmod,
 3260        ktopl   LIKE t030-ktopl,
 3261        hkont   LIKE t030-konts,
 3262      END OF ltt_acc.
 3263
 3264    STATICS:
 3265      lt_acc TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ltt_acc WITH UNIQUE KEY bklas bwmod ktop
       l.
 3266    DATA:
 3267      ls_acc TYPE ltt_acc.
 3268
 3269  * get organizational data
 3270    PERFORM f9300_read_organ
 3271      USING c_werks cs_accdet-werks.
 3272    CHECK g_s_organ-bukrs = cs_accdet-bukrs.
 3273    cs_accdet-bwkey = g_s_organ-bwkey.
 3274    cs_accdet-ktopl = g_s_organ-ktopl.
 3275    cs_accdet-bwmod = g_s_organ-bwmod.
 3276
 3277  * check for special stock that is valuated in MBEW
 3278    IF ( cs_accdet-sobkz = 'Q' OR cs_accdet-sobkz = 'E' ) AND
 3279       cs_accdet-kzbws = 'A'.
 3280      CLEAR: cs_accdet-sobkz.
 3281    ENDIF.
 3282    IF cs_accdet-sobkz = 'O' AND cs_accdet-xobew IS INITIAL.
 3283      CLEAR: cs_accdet-sobkz.
 3284    ENDIF.
 3285
 3286  * get special stock data
 3287  * (if not filled yet via customer's enhancement of type STYPE_MB5B_ADD)
 3288    CASE cs_accdet-sobkz.
 3289      WHEN 'Q'.
 3290        IF cs_accdet-mat_pspnr IS INITIAL.
 3291          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'MSEG' ''.            "n1710850
 3292          SELECT SINGLE mat_pspnr FROM mseg CONNECTION (dbcon) "n1710850
 3293            INTO cs_accdet-mat_pspnr
 3294            WHERE mblnr = cs_accdet-mblnr
 3295              AND mjahr = cs_accdet-mjahr
 3296              AND zeile = cs_accdet-zeile.
 3297        ENDIF.
 3298        CLEAR: cs_accdet-mat_kdauf, cs_accdet-mat_kdpos, cs_accdet-lifnr.
 3299      WHEN 'E'.
 3300        IF cs_accdet-mat_kdauf IS INITIAL OR cs_accdet-mat_kdpos IS INITIA
       L.
 3301          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'MSEG' ''.            "n1710850
 3302          SELECT SINGLE mat_kdauf mat_kdpos FROM mseg CONNECTION (dbcon) "
       n1710850
 3303            INTO (cs_accdet-mat_kdauf, cs_accdet-mat_kdpos)
 3304            WHERE mblnr = cs_accdet-mblnr
 3305              AND mjahr = cs_accdet-mjahr
 3306              AND zeile = cs_accdet-zeile.
 3307        ENDIF.
 3308        CLEAR: cs_accdet-mat_pspnr, cs_accdet-lifnr.
 3309      WHEN 'O'.
 3310        IF cs_accdet-lifnr IS INITIAL.
 3311          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'MSEG' ''.            "n1710850
 3312          SELECT SINGLE lifnr FROM mseg CONNECTION (dbcon)    "n1710850
 3313            INTO cs_accdet-lifnr
 3314            WHERE mblnr = cs_accdet-mblnr
 3315              AND mjahr = cs_accdet-mjahr
 3316              AND zeile = cs_accdet-zeile.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    164
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3317        ENDIF.
 3318        CLEAR: cs_accdet-mat_kdauf, cs_accdet-mat_kdpos, cs_accdet-mat_psp
       nr.
 3319      WHEN OTHERS.
 3320        CLEAR: cs_accdet-mat_kdauf, cs_accdet-mat_kdpos,
 3321               cs_accdet-mat_pspnr, cs_accdet-lifnr.
 3322    ENDCASE.
 3323
 3324  * get valuation class
 3325    PERFORM get_bklas CHANGING cs_accdet.
 3326
 3327  * get G/L account
 3328    READ TABLE lt_acc INTO ls_acc
 3329      WITH TABLE KEY bklas = cs_accdet-bklas
 3330                     bwmod = cs_accdet-bwmod
 3331                     ktopl = cs_accdet-ktopl.
 3332    IF sy-subrc = 0.
 3333      cs_accdet-hkont = ls_acc-hkont.
 3334    ELSE.
 3335      CALL FUNCTION 'MR_ACCOUNT_ASSIGNMENT'
 3336        EXPORTING
 3337          bewertungsklasse       = cs_accdet-bklas
 3338          bewertung_modif        = cs_accdet-bwmod
 3339          kontenplan             = cs_accdet-ktopl
 3340          soll_haben_kennzeichen = 'S'
 3341          vorgangsschluessel     = 'BSX'
 3342        IMPORTING
 3343          konto                  = cs_accdet-hkont
 3344        EXCEPTIONS
 3345          OTHERS                 = 5.
 3346      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
 3347        CLEAR cs_accdet-hkont.
 3348      ENDIF.
 3349      ls_acc-bklas = cs_accdet-bklas.
 3350      ls_acc-bwmod = cs_accdet-bwmod.
 3351      ls_acc-ktopl = cs_accdet-ktopl.
 3352      ls_acc-hkont = cs_accdet-hkont.
 3353      INSERT ls_acc INTO TABLE lt_acc.
 3354    ENDIF.
 3355
 3356  ENDFORM.                    " GET_ACC_DET
 3357  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3358  *&      Form  GET_BKLAS
 3359  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3360  *      Get valuation class from current settings
 3361  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 3362  FORM get_bklas  CHANGING cs_accdet  TYPE stype_accdet.
 3363
 3364    TYPES:
 3365      BEGIN OF ltt_bklas,
 3366        matnr       LIKE mbew-matnr,
 3367        bwkey       LIKE mbew-bwkey,
 3368        bwtar       LIKE mbew-bwtar,
 3369        sobkz       LIKE qbew-sobkz,
 3370        mat_pspnr   LIKE qbew-pspnr,
 3371        mat_kdauf   LIKE ebew-vbeln,
 3372        mat_kdpos   LIKE ebew-posnr,
 3373        lifnr       LIKE obew-lifnr,
 3374        bklas       LIKE mbew-bklas,
 3375      END OF ltt_bklas.
 3376
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    165
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3377    STATICS:
 3378      lt_bklas TYPE HASHED TABLE OF ltt_bklas WITH UNIQUE KEY matnr bwkey
 3379        bwtar sobkz mat_pspnr mat_kdauf mat_kdpos lifnr.
 3380    DATA:
 3381      ls_bklas TYPE ltt_bklas.
 3382
 3383    READ TABLE lt_bklas INTO ls_bklas
 3384      WITH TABLE KEY matnr     = cs_accdet-matnr
 3385                     bwkey     = cs_accdet-bwkey
 3386                     bwtar     = cs_accdet-bwtar
 3387                     sobkz     = cs_accdet-sobkz
 3388                     mat_pspnr = cs_accdet-mat_pspnr
 3389                     mat_kdauf = cs_accdet-mat_kdauf
 3390                     mat_kdpos = cs_accdet-mat_kdpos
 3391                     lifnr     = cs_accdet-lifnr.
 3392    IF sy-subrc = 0.
 3393      cs_accdet-bklas = ls_bklas-bklas.
 3394    ELSE.
 3395      CASE cs_accdet-sobkz.
 3396        WHEN 'Q'.
 3397          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'QBEW' ''.            "n1710850
 3398          SELECT SINGLE bklas FROM qbew CONNECTION (dbcon)    "n1710850
 3399            INTO cs_accdet-bklas
 3400            WHERE matnr = cs_accdet-matnr
 3401              AND bwkey = cs_accdet-bwkey
 3402              AND bwtar = cs_accdet-bwtar
 3403              AND sobkz = cs_accdet-sobkz
 3404              AND pspnr = cs_accdet-mat_pspnr.
 3405        WHEN 'E'.
 3406          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'EBEW' ''.            "n1710850
 3407          SELECT SINGLE bklas FROM ebew CONNECTION (dbcon)    "n1710850
 3408            INTO cs_accdet-bklas
 3409            WHERE matnr = cs_accdet-matnr
 3410              AND bwkey = cs_accdet-bwkey
 3411              AND bwtar = cs_accdet-bwtar
 3412              AND sobkz = cs_accdet-sobkz
 3413              AND vbeln = cs_accdet-mat_kdauf
 3414              AND posnr = cs_accdet-mat_kdpos.
 3415        WHEN 'O'.
 3416          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'OBEW' ''.            "n1710850
 3417          SELECT SINGLE bklas FROM obew CONNECTION (dbcon)    "n1710850
 3418            INTO cs_accdet-bklas
 3419            WHERE matnr = cs_accdet-matnr
 3420              AND bwkey = cs_accdet-bwkey
 3421              AND bwtar = cs_accdet-bwtar
 3422              AND sobkz = cs_accdet-sobkz
 3423              AND lifnr = cs_accdet-lifnr.
 3424        WHEN OTHERS.
 3425          PERFORM hdb_check_table USING 'MBEW' ''.            "n1710850
 3426          SELECT SINGLE bklas FROM mbew CONNECTION (dbcon)    "n1710850
 3427            INTO cs_accdet-bklas
 3428            WHERE matnr = cs_accdet-matnr
 3429              AND bwkey = cs_accdet-bwkey
 3430              AND bwtar = cs_accdet-bwtar.
 3431      ENDCASE.
 3432      ls_bklas-matnr     = cs_accdet-matnr.
 3433      ls_bklas-bwkey     = cs_accdet-bwkey.
 3434      ls_bklas-bwtar     = cs_accdet-bwtar.
 3435      ls_bklas-sobkz     = cs_accdet-sobkz.
 3436      ls_bklas-mat_pspnr = cs_accdet-mat_pspnr.
 3437      ls_bklas-mat_kdauf = cs_accdet-mat_kdauf.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_01                  Page    166
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 3438      ls_bklas-mat_kdpos = cs_accdet-mat_kdpos.
 3439      ls_bklas-lifnr     = cs_accdet-lifnr.
 3440      ls_bklas-bklas     = cs_accdet-bklas.
 3441      INSERT ls_bklas INTO TABLE lt_bklas.
 3442    ENDIF.
 3443
 3444  ENDFORM.                    " GET_BKLAS

 3446  FORM hdb_check_table  USING                                 "n1710850
 3447                        lv_tab1 TYPE tabname
 3448                        lv_tab2 TYPE tabname.
 3449
 3450  * clear dbcon, set only at the end if OK
 3451    CLEAR dbcon.
 3452
 3453    IF dbcon_active IS INITIAL.
 3454      RETURN.
 3455    ENDIF.
 3456
 3457    DATA: lt_chk_tab TYPE typ_t_tablename.
 3458
 3459    IF lv_tab1 IS NOT INITIAL.
 3460      APPEND lv_tab1 TO lt_chk_tab.
 3461    ENDIF.
 3462    IF lv_tab2 IS NOT INITIAL.
 3463      APPEND lv_tab2 TO lt_chk_tab.
 3464    ENDIF.

 3466    CALL FUNCTION c_hdb_dbcon_get
 3467      EXPORTING
 3468        i_subappl  = c_hdb_subappl
 3469  *     I_ACT_CHECK_ONLY       =

       MLBD\EHP605_RM07MLBD_FORM_02_01\' && sy-cprog ) FIELDS /cwm/cl_enh_layer
       =>get_field( ).
  198                                                              "n599218
  199  * process table BESTAND                                     "n599218
  200    LOOP AT bestand.                                          "n599218
  201      CLEAR                    l_category.                    "n599218
  202                                                              "n599218
  203  *   determine the category of each entry                    "n599218
  204
  205      IF  bestand-soll      IS INITIAL    AND
  206          bestand-haben     IS INITIAL    AND
  207          bestand-/cwm/soll      IS INITIAL    AND
  208          bestand-/cwm/haben     IS INITIAL    AND
  209          bestand-sollwert  IS INITIAL    AND
  210          bestand-habenwert IS INITIAL.
  211  *     material without movements
  212
  213        IF  bestand-endmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  214            bestand-anfmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  215            bestand-/cwm/endmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  216            bestand-/cwm/anfmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  217            bestand-anfwert   IS INITIAL  AND
  218            bestand-endwert   IS INITIAL.
  219  *       material without movements / no stocks
  220          MOVE  'PA_NDZER'     TO  l_category.
  221        ELSE.
  222  *       material without movements / with stocks
  223          MOVE  'PA_NDSTO'     TO  l_category.
  224        ENDIF.
  225      ELSE.
  226  *     material with movements
  227  *
  228        IF      bestand-anfmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  229                bestand-/cwm/anfmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  230                bestand-anfwert   IS INITIAL.
  231  *       stock and value on start date are zero
  232          IF   bestand-endmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  233               bestand-/cwm/endmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  234               bestand-endwert   IS INITIAL.
  235  *         stock and value on end date are zero, too
  236            MOVE  'PA_WDZER'     TO  l_category.
  237          ELSE.
  238  *         stock and value on end date <> zero
  239            MOVE  'PA_WDZEW'     TO  l_category.
  240          ENDIF.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    171
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  241        ELSE.
  242  *       stock and value on start date <> zero
  243          IF     bestand-endmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  244                 bestand-/cwm/endmenge  IS INITIAL  AND
  245                 bestand-endwert   IS INITIAL.
  246  *         stock and value on end date are zero
  247            MOVE  'PA_WDWIZ'   TO  l_category.
  248          ELSEIF bestand-endmenge = bestand-anfmenge  AND
  249                 bestand-/cwm/endmenge = bestand-/cwm/anfmenge  AND
  250                 bestand-anfwert  = bestand-endwert.
  251  *         stock and value on end date are equal
  252            MOVE  'PA_WDWEW'   TO  l_category.
  253          ELSE.
  254  *         stock and values on end date are differnt
  255            MOVE  'PA_WDWUW'   TO  l_category.
  256          ENDIF.
  257        ENDIF.
  258      ENDIF.
  259  *ENHANCEMENT-POINT ehp605_rm07mlbd_form_02_01 SPOTS es_rm07mlbd .
  260
  261  *   evaluate category and corresponding parameter settings  "n599218
  262      CHECK : NOT l_category IS INITIAL.                      "n599218
  263      ASSIGN (l_category)      TO  <l_fs>.                    "n599218
  264                                                              "n599218
  265      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.                                "n599218
  266        IF  <l_fs> IS INITIAL.                                "n599218
  267          DELETE               bestand.                       "n599218
  268        ENDIF.                                                "n599218
  269      ENDIF.                                                  "n599218
  270    ENDLOOP.                                                  "n599218
  271  ENDFORM.                               " LISTUMFANG

  340  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  341  *    F0000_CREATE_TABLE_G_T_ORGAN
  342  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  343
  344  FORM f0000_create_table_g_t_organ
  345                     USING     l_f_error TYPE stype_organ-keytype.
  346
  347    DATA: l_ra_bukrs TYPE RANGE OF bukrs,                     "3116194
  348          l_bukrs    TYPE bukrs.                              "3116194
  349
  350  * at least on of these 3 range tables must be filled for the creation
  351  * of table g_t_organ
  352  * field  description         range table
  353  * -----  -----------         -----------
  354  * werks  plant               g_0000_ra_plant
  355  * bwkey  valuation area      g_0000_ra_bwkey
  356  * bukrs  company code        g_0000_ra_plant
  357
  358  * table g_t_organ must be empty
  359    CHECK : g_t_organ[] IS INITIAL.                           "n433765
  360
  361  * START OF CHANGE for note 1049935                         "n1049935
  362  * First check if there was a selection for company code    "n1049935
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    173
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  363  * then do the F9000_AUTH_PLANT_CHECK only for necessary    "n1049935
  364  * selected items..                                         "n1049935
  365
  366  * select all plant from table t001w
  367    SELECT * FROM t001w        WHERE  werks  IN  g_0000_ra_werks.
  368
  369  *   check the valuation area
  370      CHECK : t001w-bwkey IN g_0000_ra_bwkey.
  371
  372  *   read the valuation area
  373      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t001k
  374                               WHERE  bwkey  =  t001w-bwkey.
  375
  376      CHECK : sy-subrc IS INITIAL.       "entry found ?
  377
  378  *   company code is required ?
  379      CHECK : t001k-bukrs IN g_0000_ra_bukrs.
  380
  381      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t001  WHERE  bukrs  =  t001k-bukrs.
  382
  383      CHECK : sy-subrc IS INITIAL.       "entry found ?
  384
  385
  386      PERFORM  f9000_auth_plant_check    USING  t001w-werks.
  387
  388  *   go on if the user has authority for this plant
  389      CHECK : g_flag_authority = 'X'.
  390  * END OF CHANGE for note 1049935                           "n1049935
  391
  392  *   create table g_t_organ_lean
  393      MOVE : t001w-werks       TO  g_s_organ-werks,
  394             t001w-bwkey       TO  g_s_organ-bwkey,
  395             t001-bukrs        TO  g_s_organ-bukrs,
  396             t001-ktopl        TO  g_s_organ-ktopl,
  397             t001k-bwmod       TO  g_s_organ-bwmod,
  398             t001-waers        TO  g_s_organ-waers.
  399
  400  *   write 2 entries for the both search methods _
  401  *   1. with key   valuation area
  402  *   2. with key   plant
  403      MOVE-CORRESPONDING  g_s_organ
  404                               TO  g_t_organ.
  405      MOVE : c_bwkey           TO  g_t_organ-keytype,
  406             g_s_organ-bwkey   TO  g_t_organ-keyfield.
  407      APPEND                   g_t_organ.
  408
  409      MOVE : c_werks           TO  g_t_organ-keytype,
  410           g_s_organ-werks     TO  g_t_organ-keyfield.
  411      APPEND                   g_t_organ.
  412      CLEAR                    g_s_organ.
  413
  414  *   create the range tables for plants
  415      MOVE : t001w-werks       TO  g_ra_werks-low,
  416             'I'               TO  g_ra_werks-sign,
  417             'EQ'              TO  g_ra_werks-option.
  418      APPEND                   g_ra_werks.
  419
  420  *   create the range tables for plants and valuation areas
  421      MOVE : t001k-bwkey       TO  g_ra_bwkey-low,
  422             'I'               TO  g_ra_bwkey-sign,
  423             'EQ'              TO  g_ra_bwkey-option.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    174
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  424      APPEND                   g_ra_bwkey.
  425
  426  *   create the range table for company codes                "3116194
  427      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO l_ra_bukrs ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_ra_bukr
       s>).
  428      MOVE : t001k-bwkey       TO  <fs_ra_bukrs>-low,
  429             'I'               TO  <fs_ra_bukrs>-sign,
  430             'EQ'              TO  <fs_ra_bukrs>-option.
  431    ENDSELECT.
  432
  433  * is table g_t_organ empty ?
  434    IF  g_t_organ[] IS INITIAL.                               "n433765
  435  *   no plants for selection found / process error message ?
  436      IF  l_f_error = c_error.
  437        MESSAGE e281.
  438  *     Kein Eintrag zur Selektion Buchungskreis Werk Lagerort vorhanden
  439      ENDIF.
  440    ELSE.
  441      SORT  g_t_organ          BY  keytype  keyfield  bwkey  werks.

  446
  447      SORT                     g_ra_bwkey.
  448      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM g_ra_bwkey.
  449    ENDIF.
