  456
  457  FORM f0300_get_fields.
  458
  459    DATA : l_f_type(01)        TYPE c.
  460
  461  * find out the fields of structure g_s_mseg_lean
  462    DESCRIBE FIELD g_s_mseg_lean INTO g_t_td.
  463
  464    LOOP AT g_t_td-names       INTO  g_s_nameinfo.
  465  *   select all entries who contain 'MSEG-' oder 'MKPF-'
  466      CASE  g_s_nameinfo-name(05).
  467        WHEN  'MKPF-'.
  468          MOVE    c_tilde      TO  g_s_nameinfo-name+4(01).
  469          MOVE    'MSEG'       TO  g_s_nameinfo-name(04).     "2963312
  470          APPEND  g_s_nameinfo-name
  471                               TO  g_t_mseg_fields.
  472
  473        WHEN  'MSEG-'.
  474          MOVE  : g_s_nameinfo-name
  475                               TO  g_s_mseg_fields-fieldname,
  476                  c_tilde      TO  g_s_mseg_fields-fieldname+4(01).
  477          APPEND  g_s_mseg_fields        TO  g_t_mseg_fields.
  478
  479        WHEN  OTHERS.
  480      ENDCASE.
  481    ENDLOOP.
  482                                                              "n1784874
  483                                                              "n599218 A
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    175
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  484    IF  g_flag_is_oil_active = 'X'.           "IS-OIL ?       "n599218 A
  485  *   the 2 IS-OIL specific data fields will be inserted into "n599218 A
  486  *   working table G_T_MSEG_FIELDS. Then these fields will   "n599218 A
  487  *   transported from database table MSEG, too               "n599218 A
  488      APPEND  'MSEG~OIGLCALC'  TO  g_t_mseg_fields.           "n599218 A
  489      APPEND  'MSEG~OIGLSKU'   TO  g_t_mseg_fields.           "n599218 A
  490    ENDIF.                                                    "n599218 A
  491                                                              "n1784874
  492
  493  * serious error if table g_t_mseg_field does not contain fields
  494    IF  g_t_mseg_fields[] IS INITIAL.                         "n599218
  495      MESSAGE e895                                     "#EC *    "n599218
  496        WITH 'Error, contact system administrator'.    "#EC *    "n599218
  497    ELSE.

  522  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
  523
  524  FORM f0600_create_range_lgort.
  525
  526    REFRESH                    g_ra_lgort.
  527    CLEAR                      g_ra_lgort.
  528
  529    IF      lgbst = 'X'.       "only Storage loc./batch stock
  530  *   copy the existing select-options
  531      MOVE lgort[]               TO  g_ra_lgort[].
  532
  533  *   add an exclusion for storage location = space
  534      MOVE : 'E'               TO  g_ra_lgort-sign,
  535             'EQ'              TO  g_ra_lgort-option.
  536      APPEND                   g_ra_lgort.
  537
  538    ELSEIF  bwbst = 'X'.       "only valuated stocks
  539  *   copy the existing select-options
  540      MOVE lgort[]               TO  g_ra_lgort[].
  541
  542    ELSEIF  sbbst = 'X'.       "only special stocks
  543      IF      sobkz  =  'O'  OR
  544              sobkz  =  'V'  OR
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    176
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  545              sobkz  =  'W'.
  546        MOVE lgort[]               TO  g_ra_lgort[].
  547  **     only records with storage location = space allowed
  548  *      MOVE : 'I'             TO  g_ra_lgort-sign,
  549  *             'EQ'            TO  g_ra_lgort-option.
  550  *      APPEND                 g_ra_lgort.
  551      ELSE.
  552  *     Copy the existing select-options
  553        MOVE lgort[]             TO  g_ra_lgort[].
  554      ENDIF.
  555    ENDIF.
  556
  557  ENDFORM.                     "f0600_create_range_lgort

 1296
 1297  FORM f1100_check_lgort_sokzg.
 1298    CLEAR                      g_flag_delete.
 1299  *   c) additional checks if valuated stock is required
 1300    IF  bwbst = 'X'.
 1301  *     check fields sobkz and kzwbs for valuated stocks
 1302      IF      g_s_mseg_lean-sobkz  =  c_space  OR
 1303              g_s_mseg_lean-sobkz  =  'O'      OR
 1304              g_s_mseg_lean-sobkz  =  'V'      OR
 1305              g_s_mseg_lean-sobkz  =  'W'.                    "n435403
 1306  *       OK : special stock indicator = ' ', 'O', 'W' or 'V'
 1307      ELSEIF  g_s_mseg_lean-kzbws = 'A'  OR
 1308              g_s_mseg_lean-kzbws = 'M'.
 1309  *       ok : document with valuated special stock
 1310      ELSE.
 1311        MOVE  'X'            TO  g_flag_delete.
 1312      ENDIF.
 1313
 1314      "{ Begin ENHO DIPCS_RM07MLBD_FORM_02 IS-AD-SSP AD_SUB }
 1315  * GA1551829
 1316  *     A&D subcontracting valuated stock = 'F' or 'R'
 1317      IF g_flag_delete IS NOT INITIAL AND
 1318         ( g_s_mseg_lean-sobkz = cl_adsub_constants=>f OR
 1319           g_s_mseg_lean-sobkz = cl_adsub_constants=>r ).
 1320        IF g_s_mseg_lean-kzbws IS NOT INITIAL.
 1321          CLEAR g_flag_delete.
 1322        ELSE.
 1323  *         before note 1592894, automatic 541 line has empty KZBWS
 1324          CALL FUNCTION 'GET_BUSTR_FROM_MOVEMENT_TYPE'

 1442                               werks = l_f_werks BINARY SEARCH.
 1443

 1445  *   plant found in buffer; take the result from the buffer
 1446      MOVE  g_t_auth_plant-ok            TO  g_flag_authority.
 1447    ELSE.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    191
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 1448  *   new plant / do the authority check / save result in buffer table
 1449      AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'M_MSEG_WMB'
 1450                      ID 'ACTVT' FIELD actvt03
 1451                      ID 'WERKS' FIELD  l_f_werks.
 1452
 1453      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1454        MOVE : 'X'             TO  g_t_auth_plant-ok,
 1455               'X'             TO  g_flag_authority.
 1456      ELSE.
 1457        CLEAR : g_t_auth_plant-ok, g_flag_authority.
 1458      ENDIF.

 1460      MOVE  l_f_werks          TO  g_t_auth_plant-werks.
 1461      APPEND                   g_t_auth_plant.
 1462      SORT                     g_t_auth_plant.
 1463    ENDIF.
 1464
 1465    IF  g_flag_authority = 'X'.
 1466      ADD  1                   TO  g_f_cnt_after.
 1467    ENDIF.

 1514  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 1515
 1516  FORM f9300_read_organ
 1517                     USING     l_f_keytype   LIKE g_s_organ-keytype
 1518                               l_f_keyfield  LIKE g_s_organ-keyfield.
 1519
 1520  * buffer
 1521    STATICS : l_s_old      TYPE      stype_organ,
 1522              l_9300_subrc LIKE      sy-subrc.
 1523
 1524    IF  l_f_keytype   =  l_s_old-keytype  AND
 1525        l_f_keyfield  =  l_s_old-keyfield.
 1526  *   the same key : take the data from the buffer
 1527      MOVE-CORRESPONDING  l_s_old        TO  g_s_organ.
 1528      MOVE  l_9300_subrc                 TO  sy-subrc.
 1529    ELSE.
 1530  *   the key has changed : read in table g_t_organ
 1531      READ TABLE g_t_organ     WITH KEY
 1532                               keytype   =  l_f_keytype
 1533                               keyfield  =  l_f_keyfield
 1534                               BINARY SEARCH.
 1535
 1536      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
 1537  *     entry found
 1538        MOVE-CORRESPONDING : g_t_organ   TO  g_s_organ,
 1539                             g_t_organ   TO  l_s_old.
 1540        CLEAR                            l_9300_subrc.
 1541      ELSE.
 1542  *     entry not found / fill the buffer
 1543        CLEAR : l_s_old,       g_s_organ.
 1544        MOVE  : sy-subrc       TO  l_9300_subrc,
 1545                l_f_keytype    TO  l_s_old-keytype,
 1546                l_f_keyfield   TO  l_s_old-keyfield.
 1547      ENDIF.
 1548    ENDIF.
 1549
 1550  ENDFORM.                     "f9300_read_organ

 1780  FORM tpc_check_get_all_cc.                                  "n486477
 1781                                                              "n486477
 1782  * select the matching plants from T001W                     "n486477
 1783    SELECT werks bwkey         FROM  t001w                    "n486477
 1784           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE g_t_t001w       "n486477
 1785           WHERE  werks IN werks.                             "n486477
 1786                                                              "n486477
 1787    IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.                                  "n486477
 1788      SORT  g_t_t001w          BY  werks bwkey.               "n486477
 1789    ELSE.                                                     "n486477
 1790      SET CURSOR               FIELD  'WERKS-LOW'.            "n486477
 1791  *   Plant & does not exist                                  "n486477
 1792      MESSAGE e892             WITH   werks-low.              "n486477
 1793    ENDIF.                                                    "n486477
 1794                                                              "n486477
 1795    LOOP AT g_t_t001w          INTO  g_s_t001w.               "n486477
 1796  *   select the matching valuation areas and comany codes    "n486477
 1797      SELECT SINGLE bwkey bukrs FROM  t001k                   "n486477
 1798           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF g_s_t001k             "n486477
 1799           WHERE  bwkey  =  g_s_t001w-bwkey                   "n486477
 1800             AND  bukrs  IN bukrs.                            "n486477
 1801                                                              "n486477
 1802      IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.                                "n486477

 2265  FORM fill_table_g_t_mseg_or                                 "n1481757
 2266              USING    uht_mmdocs_arch
 2267              CHANGING ct_mseg_key TYPE stab_mseg_xauto
 2268                       ct_mseg_or TYPE stab_mseg_xauto.
 2269    " c_ra_xauto.
 2270
 2271    DATA: lt_mseg_key       TYPE stab_mseg_xauto,
 2272          lt_mseg_key_group LIKE lt_mseg_key.
 2273
 2274    TYPES: BEGIN OF ts_group,
 2275             mblnr LIKE mkpf-mblnr,
 2276             mjahr LIKE mkpf-mjahr,
 2277           END OF ts_group.
 2278
 2279    DATA: ls_group_new TYPE ts_group,
 2280          ls_group_old TYPE ts_group.
 2281
 2282  *data: lt_ra_xauto like gt_ra_xauto.
 2283
 2284    FIELD-SYMBOLS  <ls_mseg_key> LIKE LINE OF ct_mseg_key.
 2285
 2286  *-----------------------------------------------------------------------
       *
 2287
 2288    MOVE ct_mseg_key[] TO lt_mseg_key[].
 2289    REFRESH ct_mseg_key.
 2290
 2291  *APPEND c_ra_xauto to lt_ra_xauto.
 2292
 2293    SORT lt_mseg_key BY mblnr mjahr zeile.
 2294
 2295    LOOP AT lt_mseg_key ASSIGNING <ls_mseg_key>.
 2296      MOVE-CORRESPONDING <ls_mseg_key> TO ls_group_new.
 2297
 2298      IF ls_group_new <> ls_group_old.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    205
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2299        PERFORM  fill_table_g_t_mseg_or_group
 2300  *            using     lt_ra_xauto
 2301                CHANGING  lt_mseg_key_group
 2302                          lt_mseg_key
 2303                          ct_mseg_or.
 2304
 2305        MOVE ls_group_new TO ls_group_old.
 2306        REFRESH lt_mseg_key_group.
 2307      ENDIF.
 2308
 2309      APPEND <ls_mseg_key>  TO lt_mseg_key_group.
 2310
 2311    ENDLOOP.
 2312
 2313    PERFORM  fill_table_g_t_mseg_or_group
 2314  *            using     lt_ra_xauto
 2315               CHANGING  lt_mseg_key_group
 2316                         lt_mseg_key
 2317                         ct_mseg_or.
 2318  ENDFORM.                    " FILL_TABLE_G_T_MSEG_OR         "n1481757

 2332
 2333    DATA : BEGIN OF lt_mseg_archiv  OCCURS 0.
 2334             INCLUDE STRUCTURE   mseg.
 2335    DATA : END OF lt_mseg_archiv.

 2341    READ TABLE ct_mseg_key_group INTO ls_mseg_key_group
 2342        INDEX 1.

 2344    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
 2345      RETURN.
 2346    ENDIF.

 2350                      mjahr = ls_mseg_key_group-mjahr.
 2351

 2355      APPEND LINES OF ct_mseg_key_group TO g_t_mseg_key_te.
 2356      RETURN.
 2357    ENDIF.

 2360      EXPORTING
 2361        i_archivekey   = wa_hashtable-archivekey
 2362        i_offset       = wa_hashtable-offset
 2363      TABLES
 2364        et_mseg        = lt_mseg_archiv

 2367        OTHERS         = 2.

 2370              WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
 2371    ENDIF.
 2372
 2373    LOOP AT lt_mseg_archiv ASSIGNING <ls_mseg_archiv>.
 2374
 2375      READ TABLE ct_mseg_key_group INTO ls_mseg_key_group
 2376        WITH KEY mblnr = <ls_mseg_archiv>-mblnr
 2377                 mjahr = <ls_mseg_archiv>-mjahr

 2384        MOVE-CORRESPONDING <ls_mseg_archiv> TO ls_mseg_or.

 2390      ENDIF.

 2394  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 2395  *& Form fetch_mblnr_wh_styp
 2396  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 2397  *& text
 2398  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 2399  *& -->  p1        text
 2400  *& <--  p2        text
 2401  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 2402  FORM fetch_mblnr_wh_styp .
 2403  *  SELECT DISTINCT ordim~lgnum, ordim~nltyp, ordim~mmim_mblnr AS ordim_m
       blnr, ordim~mmim_mjahr AS ordim_mjahr, ordim~mmim_zeile AS ordim_zeile,
 2404  *                mseg~mblnr AS refdoc_mblnr, mseg~mjahr AS refdoc_mjahr,
        mseg~zeile AS refdoc_zeile
 2405  *  FROM /scwm/ordim_c AS ordim
 2406  *  LEFT OUTER JOIN /scdl/db_refdoc AS refdoc
 2407  *  ON refdoc~docid EQ ordim~rdocid
 2408  *  LEFT OUTER JOIN matdoc AS mseg
 2409  *  ON mseg~xblnr EQ refdoc~refdocno
 2410  *  INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_mblnr_all)
 2411  *  WHERE ordim~lgnum IN @lgnum
 2412  *    AND ordim~nltyp IN @lgtyp.
 2413  *
 2414  *  gt_mblnr = VALUE #(
 2415  *    FOR ls_mblnr IN lt_mblnr_all
 2416  *    ( lgnum = ls_mblnr-lgnum
 2417  *      nltyp = ls_mblnr-nltyp
 2418  *      mblnr = ls_mblnr-ordim_mblnr
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15.09.26 DEV      Include ZMM_RM07MLBD_FORM_02                  Page    207
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2419  *      mjahr = ls_mblnr-ordim_mjahr
 2420  *      zeile = ls_mblnr-ordim_zeile )
 2421  *
 2422  **      FOR ls_mblnr IN lt_mblnr_all
 2423  *    ( lgnum = ls_mblnr-lgnum
 2424  *      nltyp = ls_mblnr-nltyp
 2425  *      mblnr = ls_mblnr-refdoc_mblnr
 2426  *      mjahr = ls_mblnr-refdoc_mjahr
 2427  *      zeile = ls_mblnr-refdoc_zeile )
 2428  **      ( ls_mblnr-refdoc_mblnr )
 2429  *  ).
 2430  *
 2431  *  DELETE gt_mblnr WHERE mblnr IS INITIAL AND mjahr IS INITIAL AND zeile
        IS INITIAL.
 2432  *  SORT gt_mblnr BY mblnr mjahr zeile.
 2433  *  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_mblnr.
 2434  ENDFORM.
